# 1048 new apps — 2026-11-09

**Anomaly:** 1048 new apps is 689% of the median of the last 5 reports (152). Check for duplicates or a change in what Apple returns.

Window: apps first found between 2026-11-08 17:16 UTC (18:16 Europe/Warsaw) and 2026-11-09 19:19 UTC (20:19 Europe/Warsaw) and released since 2026-11-07 17:16 UTC (18:16 Europe/Warsaw), plus apps found earlier whose release date arrived in that span. Date-only release dates count by calendar day in America/Los_Angeles. Storefronts: us.
